local function new(class, props, parent)
	local o = Instance.new(class)
	for k, v in pairs(props) do o[k] = v end
	if parent then o.Parent = parent end
	return o
end
local function corner(o, r) return new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r) }, o) end
local function round(o) return new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, o) end
local function stroke(o, color, thickness, transparency)
	return new("UIStroke", { Color = color, Thickness = thickness or 1, Transparency = transparency or 0, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, o)
end
local function text(parent, props)
	local l = new("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Font = FONT, TextColor3 = C.text, TextSize = 13, Text = "",
	}, parent)
	for k, v in pairs(props) do l[k] = v end
	return l
end
local function at(x, y) return UDim2.fromOffset(x * S, y * S) end
local function px(w, h) return UDim2.fromOffset(w * S, h * S) end

local function mount(gui)
	local ok = false
	if gethui then ok = pcall(function() gui.Parent = gethui() end) and gui.Parent ~= nil end
	if not ok then ok = pcall(function() gui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end) end
	if not ok then gui.Parent = Players.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end
end

local ui = {}
ui.gui = new("ScreenGui", { Name = "YGJHub", ResetOnSpawn = false, DisplayOrder = 100, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling })
mount(ui.gui)

local function viewport()
	local ok, w, h = pcall(function()
		local s = ui.gui.AbsoluteSize
		return s.X, s.Y
	end)
	if ok and type(w) == "number" and type(h) == "number" and w > 0 and h > 0 then return w, h end
	return 1280, 720
end

local CURSOR_STEP = "YGJHubCursor"
local savedIcon = nil
local function forceCursor() UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true end
local function setCursor(show)
	if show then
		if savedIcon ~= nil then return end
		local ok, v = pcall(function() return UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled end)
		if not ok or type(v) ~= "boolean" then return end
		savedIcon = v
		pcall(function() RunService:BindToRenderStep(CURSOR_STEP, Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value, forceCursor) end)
		pcall(forceCursor)
	elseif savedIcon ~= nil then
		pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(CURSOR_STEP) end)
		local before = savedIcon
		savedIcon = nil
		pcall(function() UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = before end)
	end
end

local win = { x = 0, y = 0, scale = 1 }
local minimized = false

local function designSize() return DESIGN_W * win.scale, DESIGN_H * win.scale end
local function maxScale(w, h) return math.max(MIN_SCALE, math.min(MAX_SCALE, (w - 16) / (DESIGN_W * S), (h - 16) / (DESIGN_H * S))) end

local function applyWindow()
	local w, h = viewport()
	win.scale = math.clamp(win.scale, MIN_SCALE, maxScale(w, h))
	local bw, bh = designSize()
	win.x = math.clamp(win.x, 0, math.max(0, w - bw))
	win.y = math.clamp(win.y, 0, math.max(0, h - bh))
	if ui.root then
		ui.root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(bw, bh)
		ui.root.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(win.x, win.y)
		ui.scale.Scale = S * win.scale
		if minimized then ui.root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(bw, TITLE_H * S * win.scale) end
	end
end

local function placeWindow(useSaved)
	local w, h = viewport()
	win.scale = math.clamp((useSaved and saved.window.scale) or 1, MIN_SCALE, maxScale(w, h))
	if useSaved and saved.window.x then
		win.x, win.y = saved.window.x, saved.window.y
	else
		local bw, bh = DESIGN_W * S * win.scale, DESIGN_H * S * win.scale
		win.x, win.y = (w - bw) / 2, (h - bh) / 2
	end
	applyWindow()
end
local function rememberWindow()
	saved.window = { x = win.x, y = win.y, scale = win.scale, layout = LAYOUT }
	touch()
end
local function resetWindow() placeWindow(false) rememberWindow() end

local function statusContent()
	local stats = env.RivalsStats
	if not stats then return "아직 기록 없음" end
	local lines = {}
	local judged = (stats.windowHits or 0) + (stats.windowMisses or 0)
	if judged > 0 then
		table.insert(lines, string.format("명중 %d/%d (%d%%)", stats.windowHits, judged, math.floor(100 * stats.windowHits / judged + 0.5)))
	else
		table.insert(lines, string.format("명중 %d/%d", stats.hits or 0, (stats.hits or 0) + (stats.misses or 0)))
	end
	if (stats.headTotal or 0) > 0 then
		table.insert(lines, string.format("헤드 %d/%d (%d%%)", stats.headHits, stats.headTotal, math.floor(100 * stats.headHits / stats.headTotal + 0.5)))
	end
	local check = env.RivalsCheck
	if check then
		local bad = 0
		for _, item in ipairs(check.items) do if not item.ok then bad += 1 end end
		table.insert(lines, bad == 0 and "에임 점검 정상" or ("에임 점검 문제 " .. bad))
	end
	local safety = env.RivalsSafety
	if safety then
		if safety.rangeScale < 1 then table.insert(lines, string.format("사거리 x%.2f", safety.rangeScale)) end
		if safety.jitterScale < 1 then table.insert(lines, string.format("튕김 x%.2f", safety.jitterScale)) end
	end
	return table.concat(lines, "\n")
end

local function recordContent()
	local lines = { env.RivalsVersion or "체크포인트 ?" }
	local stats = env.RivalsStats
	if stats and type(stats.log) == "table" then
		table.insert(lines, string.format("샷 %d · 명중 %d/%d", #stats.log, stats.windowHits or 0, (stats.windowHits or 0) + (stats.windowMisses or 0)))
	end
	local events = env.RivalsEvents
	if events and type(events.list) == "table" then table.insert(lines, "이벤트 " .. #events.list) end
	return table.concat(lines, "\n")
end

local function unloadAll()
	if env.RivalsUnloadAll then env.RivalsUnloadAll() end
end

local function petSettings()
	saved.pet = saved.pet or {}
	return saved.pet
end

	b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(b, 0.12, { BackgroundColor3 = onColor, TextColor3 = C.bg0 }) end)
	b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(b, 0.12, { BackgroundColor3 = C.card, TextColor3 = C.dim }) end)
end

local BODY_X = SIDEBAR_W + 14

local body = new("Frame", { Name = "Body", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = at(BODY_X, TITLE_H + 12), Size = px(BODY_W, DESIGN_H - TITLE_H - 24), ZIndex = 4 }, ui.root)

local refreshers = {}

local function addToggle(parent, order, row)
	local h = row.detail and 50 or 40
	local frame = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = C.card, BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = order, Size = px(BODY_W, h), ZIndex = 4 }, parent)
	corner(frame, 9)
	stroke(frame, C.line, 1, 0)
	new("Frame", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, (h - 22) / 2 * S), Size = px(3, 22), BackgroundColor3 = row.color or C.neon, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 5 }, frame)
	corner(new("Frame", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, (h - 22) / 2 * S), Size = px(3, 22), BackgroundColor3 = row.color or C.neon, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 5 }, frame), 2)
	local dot = new("Frame", { Position = at(16, row.detail and 16 or (h - 8) / 2), Size = px(8, 8), BackgroundColor3 = row.color or C.neon, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 5 }, frame)
	round(dot)
	text(frame, { Position = at(34, row.detail and 8 or 0), Size = px(BODY_W - 120, row.detail and 20 or h), TextSize = 12, Text = row.label, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ZIndex = 5 })
	if row.detail then
		text(frame, { Position = at(34, 26), Size = px(BODY_W - 120, 14), TextSize = 10, Text = row.detail, TextColor3 = C.dim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ZIndex = 5 })
	end
	local track = new("TextButton", { Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -12 * S, 0.5, 0), Size = px(40, 22), BackgroundColor3 = C.line, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 5 }, frame)
	round(track)
	local tint = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = row.color or C.neon, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 5 }, track)
	round(tint)
	local knob = new("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 2 * S, 0.5, 0), Size = px(18, 18), BackgroundColor3 = C.text, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 6 }, track)
	round(knob)
	local shown = nil
	local function refresh()
		local want = row.get() == true
		if shown == want then return end
		shown = want
		local p = want and 1 or 0
		tween(knob, 0.15, { Position = UDim2.new(0, (2 + p * (40 - 4 - 18)) * S, 0.5, 0) })
		tween(tint, 0.15, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 - p })
	end
	track.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() row.set(not (row.get() == true)) shown = nil refresh() end)
	track.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(track, 0.12, { BackgroundColor3 = C.lineHi }) end)
	track.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(track, 0.12, { BackgroundColor3 = C.line }) end)
	table.insert(refreshers, refresh)
	refresh()
end

local function addSegment(parent, order, row)
	local frame = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = C.card, BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = order, Size = px(BODY_W, 46), ZIndex = 4 }, parent)
	corner(frame, 9)
	stroke(frame, C.line, 1, 0)
	new("Frame", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 12 * S), Size = px(3, 22), BackgroundColor3 = row.color or C.neon, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 5 }, frame)
	corner(new("Frame", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 12 * S), Size = px(3, 22), BackgroundColor3 = row.color or C.neon, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 5 }, frame), 2)
	text(frame, { Position = at(16, 0), Size = px(120, 46), TextSize = 12, Text = row.label, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ZIndex = 5 })
	local width, count = 220, #row.options
	local seg = new("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -12 * S, 0.5, 0), Size = px(width, 26), BackgroundColor3 = C.cardHi, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 5 }, frame)
	corner(seg, 7)
	local slot = (width - 4) / count
	local chip = new("Frame", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(2 * S, 2 * S), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(slot * S, 22 * S), BackgroundColor3 = row.color or C.neon, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 5 }, seg)
	corner(chip, 5)
	local buttons, shown = {}, nil
	local function refresh()
		local want = row.get()
		if shown == want then return end
		shown = want
		for index, entry in ipairs(buttons) do
			local on = entry.value == want
			entry.btn.Font = on and FONT_BOLD or FONT
			entry.btn.TextColor3 = on and C.bg0 or C.dim
			if on then tween(chip, 0.15, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset((2 + (index - 1) * slot) * S, 2 * S) }) end
		end
	end
	for index, option in ipairs(row.options) do
		local b = new("TextButton", {
			Text = option[2], AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset((2 + (index - 1) * slot) * S, 2 * S), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(slot * S, 22 * S),
			Font = FONT, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = C.dim, ZIndex = 6,
		}, seg)
		b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() row.set(option[1]) shown = nil refresh() end)
		table.insert(buttons, { btn = b, value = option[1] })
	end
	table.insert(refreshers, refresh)
	refresh()
end

local function addButton(parent, order, row)
	local btn = new("TextButton", { Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = C.card, BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = order, Size = px(BODY_W, 40), ZIndex = 4 }, parent)
	corner(btn, 9)
	stroke(btn, C.line, 1, 0)
	text(btn, { Position = at(16, 0), Size = px(BODY_W - 32, 40), TextSize = 12, Text = row.label, TextColor3 = row.color or C.neon, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ZIndex = 5 })
	btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(btn, 0.12, { BackgroundColor3 = C.cardHi }) end)
	btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(btn, 0.12, { BackgroundColor3 = C.card }) end)
	btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		local ok, err = pcall(row.run)
		if not ok then warn("[YGJ] " .. tostring(err)) end
		if refreshAll then refreshAll() end
	end)
end

local function addText(parent, order, row, card)
	local frame = new("Frame", {
		BackgroundColor3 = card and C.card or C.cardHi, BackgroundTransparency = card and 0 or 0.3, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		LayoutOrder = order, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ZIndex = 4,
	}, parent)
	corner(frame, 9)
	if card then stroke(frame, C.line, 1, 0) end
	new("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 10 * S), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 10 * S), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 14 * S), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 14 * S) }, frame)
	local label = text(frame, {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, TextSize = card and 12 or 10,
		TextTransparency = card and 0 or 0.35, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
		TextWrapped = true, LineHeight = 1.25, ZIndex = 5,
	})
	local shown = nil
	local function refresh()
		local value = row.text()
		if value ~= shown then shown = value label.Text = value end
	end
	table.insert(refreshers, refresh)
	refresh()
end

local bodyPages = {}
for _, tab in ipairs(TABS) do
	local page = new("ScrollingFrame", {
		Name = "Page_" .. tab.id, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false,
		Position = at(0, 0), Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
		AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollBarThickness = 3, ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.neon, ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.5,
		ZIndex = 4,
	}, body)
	new("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 8 * S), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, page)
	bodyPages[tab.id] = page
	for order, row in ipairs(tab.rows) do
		if row.type == "toggle" then addToggle(page, order, row)
		elseif row.type == "segment" then addSegment(page, order, row)
		elseif row.type == "button" then addButton(page, order, row)
		else addText(page, order, row, row.type == "info") end
	end
end

local currentTab = nil
local function selectTab(id)
	currentTab = id
	for key, data in pairs(tabButtons) do
		local on = key == id
		tween(data.btn, 0.15, { BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1 })
		data.label.TextColor3 = on and data.color or C.dim
		data.label.Font = on and FONT_BOLD or FONT_MED
		data.ind.Visible = on
	end
	for key, page in pairs(bodyPages) do page.Visible = key == id end
	if refreshAll then refreshAll() end
end

for id, data in pairs(tabButtons) do
	data.btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() selectTab(id) end)
	data.btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		if currentTab ~= id then tween(data.btn, 0.12, { BackgroundColor3 = C.cardHi, BackgroundTransparency = 0 }) end
	end)
	data.btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		if currentTab ~= id then tween(data.btn, 0.12, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }) end
	end)
end

function refreshAll()
	for _, refresh in ipairs(refreshers) do refresh() end
end

local isOpen = false
local function setOpen(open)
	isOpen = open
	pcall(function() ui.gui.Enabled = open end)
	cfg.menuOpen = open
	setCursor(open)
	if open then
		selectTab(currentTab or "aim")
		refreshAll()
	end
	if dirty then save() end
end

closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setOpen(false) end)

minBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	minimized = not minimized
	if minimized then
		minBtn.Text = "□"
		tween(ui.root, 0.2, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(DESIGN_W * S * win.scale, TITLE_H * S * win.scale) })
		sidebar.Visible = false
		body.Visible = false
	else
		minBtn.Text = "–"
		tween(ui.root, 0.2, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(DESIGN_W * S * win.scale, DESIGN_H * S * win.scale) })
		sidebar.Visible = true
		body.Visible = true
	end
end)

local drag = nil
local function pressed(input)
	local k = input.UserInputType
	return k == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or k == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
end
local function moved(input)
	local k = input.UserInputType
	return k == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or k == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
end

titlebar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if pressed(input) and not drag then
		local p = input.Position
		drag = { px = p.X, py = p.Y, x0 = win.x, y0 = win.y }
	end
end)

table.insert(connections, UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
	if not drag or not moved(input) then return end
	local p = input.Position
	win.x = drag.x0 + (p.X - drag.px)
	win.y = drag.y0 + (p.Y - drag.py)
	applyWindow()
end))

table.insert(connections, UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
	if not pressed(input) or not drag then return end
	drag = nil
	rememberWindow()
end))

placeWindow(true)
minimized = false
setCursor(false)
selectTab("aim")
ui.gui.Enabled = false

local pollClock = 0
local lastW, lastH = viewport()
table.insert(connections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
	pollClock += dt or 0
	if dirty and env.RivalsClock() - lastChange >= SAVE_AFTER then save() end
	if pollClock >= 0.15 then
		pollClock = 0
		if isOpen then refreshAll() end
		local w, h = viewport()
		if w ~= lastW or h ~= lastH then
			lastW, lastH = w, h
			applyWindow()
		end
	end
end))

table.insert(connections, UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
	if processed then return end
	if input.KeyCode == TOGGLE_KEY then setOpen(not isOpen)
	elseif input.KeyCode == UNLOAD_KEY then unloadAll() end
end))

env.RivalsMenu = {
	gui = ui.gui,
	window = win,
	settings = saved,
	Open = function(_, open) setOpen(open) end,
	Toggle = function() setOpen(not isOpen) end,
	Save = function() return save() end,
	Touch = function() touch() end,
	ResetWindow = function() resetWindow() end,
	Features = FEATURE_KEYS,
}

env.RivalsMenuUnload = function()
	for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do connection:Disconnect() end
	if dirty then save() end
	cfg.menuOpen = false
	setCursor(false)
	env.RivalsMenu = nil
	ui.gui:Destroy()
end

env.RivalsUnloadAll = function()
	for _, name in ipairs({ "RivalsAutoFireUnload", "RivalsAntiAimUnload", "RivalsEspUnload", "RivalsAimUnload", "RivalsBaseUnload", "RivalsPetUnload" }) do
		local unload = env[name]
		if type(unload) == "function" then pcall(unload) end
	end
	warn("[YGJ] 전부 껐다. 다시 쓰려면 스크립트를 재실행해라")
	pcall(env.RivalsMenuUnload)
end

end)
--==============================================================
--  [8] PET  ·  "고양이가 욕한다 (강제 설정 없음)"
--==============================================================
run("pet", function()

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local env = getgenv()
if env.RivalsPetUnload then pcall(env.RivalsPetUnload) end
local cfg = env.RivalsCfg
if not cfg then return end

local NEAR_DEATH = 0.3
local RECOVERED = 0.6

local menu = env.RivalsMenu
local settings = (menu and menu.settings) or {}
settings.pet = settings.pet or {}
local P = settings.pet
if type(P.show) ~= "boolean" then P.show = true end
if type(P.chatter) ~= "number" then P.chatter = 2 end
if type(P.swear) ~= "boolean" then P.swear = true end

local function touch()
	if menu and menu.Touch then menu.Touch() end
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- 이미지 디코드 / 에셋
----------------------------------------------------------------
local B64 = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
local function b64decode(data)
	local map = {}
	for index = 1, 64 do map[string.byte(B64, index)] = index - 1 end
	local out, buffer, bits = {}, 0, 0
	for index = 1, #data do
		local value = map[string.byte(data, index)]
		if value then
			buffer = buffer * 64 + value
			bits += 6
			if bits >= 8 then
				bits -= 8
				local divisor = 2 ^ bits
				table.insert(out, string.char(math.floor(buffer / divisor) % 256))
				buffer = buffer % divisor
			end
		end
	end
	return table.concat(out)
end

local function catAsset()
	local loader = getcustomasset or getsynasset
	if type(writefile) ~= "function" or type(loader) ~= "function" then return nil end
	local payload = CAT_PNG
	if type(payload) ~= "string" or payload == "" or string.find(payload, "여기에") then return nil end
	local ok, asset = pcall(function()
		if type(isfile) ~= "function" or not isfile(CAT_FILE) then writefile(CAT_FILE, b64decode(payload)) end
		return loader(CAT_FILE)
	end)
	if ok and type(asset) == "string" and asset ~= "" then return asset end
	return nil
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- GUI
----------------------------------------------------------------
local connections = {}
local function new(class, props, parent)
	local o = Instance.new(class)
	for k, v in pairs(props) do o[k] = v end
	if parent then o.Parent = parent end
	return o
end

local gui = new("ScreenGui", { Name = "YGJPet", ResetOnSpawn = false, DisplayOrder = 99, Enabled = P.show, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling })
local mounted = false
if gethui then mounted = pcall(function() gui.Parent = gethui() end) and gui.Parent ~= nil end
if not mounted then mounted = pcall(function() gui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end) end
if not mounted then gui.Parent = Players.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end

local function viewport()
	local ok, w, h = pcall(function()
		local s = gui.AbsoluteSize
		return s.X, s.Y
	end)
	if ok and type(w) == "number" and type(h) == "number" and w > 0 and h > 0 then return w, h end
	return 1280, 720
end

local petButton = new("TextButton", { Name = "Pet", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(PET_W, PET_H), ZIndex = 5 }, gui)
local asset = catAsset()
local picture = new("ImageLabel", { Name = "Picture", BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Image = asset or "", Visible = asset ~= nil, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit, ZIndex = 5 }, petButton)
local fallback = new("TextLabel", { Name = "Fallback", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Text = "🐱", TextSize = 56, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Visible = asset == nil, ZIndex = 5 }, petButton)

local bubble = new("Frame", { Name = "Bubble", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), BorderSizePixel = 0, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Visible = false, ZIndex = 8 }, gui)
new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12) }, bubble)
new("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 8), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 12), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 12) }, bubble)
new("UISizeConstraint", { MinSize = Vector2.new(60, 0) }, bubble)
local bubbleText = new("TextLabel", { Name = "Text", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, TextSize = 15, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 34), TextWrapped = true, Text = "", ZIndex = 9 }, bubble)
new("UISizeConstraint", { MaxSize = Vector2.new(206, 400) }, bubbleText)
local bubbleScale = new("UIScale", { Scale = 1 }, bubble)
local tail = new("Frame", { Name = "Tail", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12), Rotation = 45, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 7 }, bubble)

local pos = { x = 0, y = 0 }
local bobOffset = 0

local function clampPet()
	local w, h = viewport()
	pos.x = math.clamp(pos.x, 0, math.max(0, w - PET_W))
	pos.y = math.clamp(pos.y, 0, math.max(0, h - PET_H))
end

local function applyPet()
	clampPet()
	petButton.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(pos.x, pos.y + bobOffset)
	local w, h = viewport()
	local center = math.clamp(pos.x + PET_W / 2, 120, math.max(120, w - 120))
	if pos.y < 90 then
		bubble.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
		bubble.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(center, pos.y + PET_H + 12)
		tail.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 2)
	else
		bubble.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
		bubble.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(center, pos.y - 10)
		tail.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -2)
	end
end

local function defaultPosition()
	local w, h = viewport()
	pos.x, pos.y = w - PET_W - 28, h - PET_H - 28
end

local function placePet()
	if type(P.x) == "number" and type(P.y) == "number" then pos.x, pos.y = P.x, P.y
	else defaultPosition() end
	applyPet()
end

local drag = nil
local function pressed(input)
	local k = input.UserInputType
	return k == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or k == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
end
local function moving(input)
	local k = input.UserInputType
	return k == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or k == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- 대사 엔진
----------------------------------------------------------------
local busyUntil, busyPriority = 0, 0
local hideAt = 0
local lastLine = {}
local hop = 0

local function pick(name, lines)
	local index = math.random(#lines)
	if #lines > 1 and index == lastLine[name] then index = index % #lines + 1 end
	lastLine[name] = index
	return lines[index]
end

local BUBBLE_KEY = {}
local hiding = false
local function showBubble(p)
	local alpha = math.clamp(p, 0, 1)
	bubbleScale.Scale = 0.6 + 0.4 * p
	bubble.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - alpha
	bubbleText.TextTransparency = 1 - alpha
	tail.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - alpha
end
local function popBubble()
	hiding = false
	local motion = env.RivalsMotion
	if not motion then showBubble(1) return end
	if bubble.Visible and motion.values[BUBBLE_KEY] == 1 then motion.set(BUBBLE_KEY, 0.8, showBubble)
	elseif not bubble.Visible then motion.set(BUBBLE_KEY, 0, showBubble) end
	motion.spring(BUBBLE_KEY, 1, { duration = 0.38, bounce = 0.3, eps = 0.001 }, showBubble)
end
local function hideBubble()
	if hiding then return end
	hiding = true
	local motion = env.RivalsMotion
	if not motion then bubble.Visible = false return end
	motion.spring(BUBBLE_KEY, 0, { duration = 0.25, eps = 0.001 }, showBubble, function() bubble.Visible = false hiding = false end)
end

	hackConfirm = { "{name} 진짜 핵이네 ㅅㅂ 어이없네", "내가 {name} 핵이라 했지 씨발", "다음 판 {name} 또 뜨면 ㅈㄴ 조심해" },
	curse = { "아 진짜 어이없네 ㅅㅂ 다시 가자", "이번엔 ㄹㅇ 정신 차릴게", "그래도 괜찮아 씨발 다음 판 있잖아" },
	loseRage = { "다음 판엔 진짜 갚아준다 씨발", "아직 안 끝났잖아 정신 차려 ㅅㅂ", "이번 건 그냥 존나 운 없었던 거임" },
	winRage = { "이 텐션 그대로 쭉 가자 ㅅㅂ", "오늘 너 존나 잘한다 ㄹㅇ", "다음 판도 씹어먹자 ㅋㅋㅋ" },
	suggestRage = { "레이지는 메뉴 에임에서 바로 켜지거든 ㅅㅂ", "켜고 이 판 존나 뒤집자 ㅅㅂ", "싫으면 끄면 되잖아 일단 한번 켜 봐 ㅅㅂ" },
	suggestAntiAim = { "안티에임은 메뉴 안티에임에서 켜면 된다고 ㅅㅂ", "강도는 약부터 올려 봐 ㅈㄴ 괜찮아", "켜 놓고 딱 한 판만 해 봐 ㅋㅋ" },
}
local lastTopic, lastTopicAt = nil, 0
local talkVars
local function fillVars(text, vars)
	return (string.gsub(text, "{(%w+)}", function(key)
		local value = vars[key]
		if value == nil then return "" end
		return tostring(value)
	end))
end

local SOFT_STEMS = {
	"쩐", "쩔", "쩌", "째", "좋", "꿀", "웃", "못", "빡", "짜", "이득", "이겼",
	"이김", "이기", "지리", "지림", "지린", "쉽", "아깝", "아쉽", "아파", "멋", "대박", "대단",
	"잘", "든든", "신나", "신난", "빠르", "빠름", "굿", "무섭", "무서", "위험", "시원", "괜찮",
	"가볍", "사기", "재밌", "세게", "피곤", "팽팽", "뿌듯", "건방", "올랐", "괴물", "승", "털",
	"깔끔", "짜릿", "쾌감", "억울", "강해", "강하", "허무", "미워", "미쳤", "망함", "놀랐", "놀람",
	"쌓", "줄었", "싸운", "환영", "정확", "편하", "구려", "달라", "헛짓", "조심", "티남", "많",
	"약", "짧", "민폐", "기분", "답답", "별로",
}
local SOFT_WORDS = {
	{ "씨발", "" }, { "시발", "" }, { "씨팔", "" }, { "씨바", "" }, { "ㅆㅂ", "" }, { "ㅅㅂ", "" },
	{ "존나", "진짜" }, { "졸라", "진짜" }, { "ㅈㄴ", "진짜" }, { "엿같", "별로" }, { "지랄", "난리" }, { "닥쳐", "조용히 해" },
	{ "꺼져", "저리 가" }, { "새끼", "녀석" }, { "뒤졌", "죽었" }, { "뒤질", "죽을" }, { "뒤져", "죽어" }, { "뒤지", "죽" },
	{ "미쳤다", "대박이다" }, { "미쳤네", "대박이네" }, { "미쳤어", "대박이야" }, { "미쳤냐", "대박이냐" }, { "미쳤나", "대박인가" }, { "미쳤지", "대박이지" },
	{ "미쳤고", "대박이고" }, { "미쳤", "대박이" }, { "미친", "대박" }, { "빡쳐", "열받아" }, { "빡치네", "열받네" }, { "빡친다", "열받는다" },
	{ "빡쳤", "열받았" }, { "빡치게", "열받게" }, { "빡친", "열받은" }, { "빡치", "열받" },
}
local function soften(text)
	for _, stem in ipairs(SOFT_STEMS) do text = string.gsub(text, "개" .. stem, stem) end
	for _, pair in ipairs(SOFT_WORDS) do text = string.gsub(text, pair[1], pair[2]) end
	text = string.gsub(text, "%s+", " ")
	text = string.gsub(text, "^[%s,%.!]+", "")
	text = string.gsub(text, "[%s,]+$", "")
	text = string.gsub(text, " ([,%.!%?])", "%1")
	local core = string.gsub(text, "%%s", "x")
	core = string.gsub(core, "{%w+}", "x")
	core = string.gsub(core, "[%s%p]", "")
	for _, mark in ipairs({ "ㅋ", "ㅎ", "ㅠ", "ㅜ" }) do core = string.gsub(core, mark, "") end
	if (utf8.len(core) or 0) < 2 then return nil end
	return text
end

local function say(name, extra, vars)
	local situation = SITUATIONS[name]
	if not situation or not P.show then return false end
	if (P.chatter or 2) < situation.level then return false end
	local now = env.RivalsClock()
	if situation.cooldown and lastSaid and lastSaid[name] and now - lastSaid[name] < situation.cooldown then return false end
	if now < busyUntil and situation.priority <= busyPriority then return false end
	local text = pick(name, situation.lines)
	local follow = lastTopic and FOLLOW_UP[lastTopic]
	if follow and name == lastTopic and now - lastTopicAt < 25 and math.random() < 0.35 then
		text = follow[math.random(#follow)]
	end
	if P.swear == false then
		local soft = soften(text)
		local tries = 0
		while soft == nil and tries < 8 do
			tries += 1
			soft = soften(pick(name, situation.lines))
		end
		if soft == nil then return false end
		text = soft
	end
	if FOLLOW_UP[name] then lastTopic, lastTopicAt = name, now end
	if extra ~= nil then text = string.format(text, extra) end
	if string.find(text, "{", 1, true) then text = fillVars(text, talkVars and talkVars(vars) or {}) end
	lastSaid = lastSaid or {}
	lastSaid[name] = now
	bubbleText.Text = text
	popBubble()
	bubble.Visible = true
	local length = utf8.len(text) or #text
	busyUntil = now + 1.6 + length * 0.07
	busyPriority = situation.priority
	hideAt = now + 2.8 + length * 0.12
	hop = 1
	applyPet()
	return true
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- 상태 감시
----------------------------------------------------------------
local watch = {
	cursor = 0, matchId = nil,
	kills = 0, deaths = 0, taken = 0,
	lifeTaken = 0, lowest = 1, low = false, hold = false, fraction = 1,
	hits = nil, misses = nil, hitStreak = 0, missStreak = 0, hitRun = 0, missRun = 0, matchHits = 0, matchMisses = 0,
	features = {}, open = false, nextIdle = 0,
	killTimes = {}, killStreak = 0, deathStreak = 0, killsOn = {}, said = {},
	roundAt = 0, matchAt = 0, lastDeathAt = -math.huge, lastKillAt = -math.huge, lastMeleeAt = -math.huge, lastDesyncAt = -math.huge,
	lastHurtAt = 0, lastActivity = 0, meleeCount = 0, chips = {}, wasHurt = false, lowSince = nil,
	winStreak = 0, loseStreak = 0, shotsSeen = nil, pendingRecords = {}, weapon = nil, target = nil,
	menuAt = nil, level = nil, chatter = nil, sink = nil, shield = nil, triggerHits = nil, pokes = {}, started = env.RivalsClock(),
	matchCount = 0, lastDist = nil, sudden = {}, seen = {}, nextTalk = 0,
	roundKills = 0, roundDeaths = 0,
}

talkVars = function(extra)
	local stats = env.RivalsStats
	local finished = watch.matchHits + watch.matchMisses
	local ping = stats and stats.Ping and stats:Ping()
	local vars = {
		name = watch.target or "쟤",
		kills = watch.kills, deaths = watch.deaths, matches = watch.matchCount,
		acc = finished > 0 and math.floor(100 * watch.matchHits / finished + 0.5) or 0,
		ping = ping and math.floor(ping + 0.5) or "?",
		dist = watch.lastDist and string.format("%.0f", watch.lastDist) or "?",
	}
	for key, value in pairs(extra or {}) do vars[key] = value end
	return vars
end

local LEVEL_NAMES = { "약", "보통", "강", "최강" }

local function hourNow()
	local ok, now = pcall(os.date, "*t")
	if ok and type(now) == "table" and type(now.hour) == "number" then return now.hour end
	return nil
end

local function weaponKind(name)
	name = tostring(name or "")
	if string.find(name, "Sniper", 1, true) then return "sniper" end
	if string.find(name, "Shotgun", 1, true) then return "shotgun" end
	if name == "RPG" or string.find(name, "Grenade", 1, true) then return "explosive" end
	if name == "Revolver" or name == "Handgun" or name == "Exogun" or string.find(name, "Pistol", 1, true) then return "pistol" end
	return nil
end

local function lastHitOn(name)
	local stats = env.RivalsStats
	if not stats or type(stats.log) ~= "table" then return nil end
	local log = stats.log
	for index = #log, math.max(1, #log - 600), -1 do
		local record = log[index]
		if record.result == "hit" and record.target and record.target.Name == name then return record end
	end
	return nil
end

local function sayEntry(entry)
	if type(entry[3]) == "table" then return say(entry[1], nil, entry[3]) end
	return say(entry[1], entry[3])
end
local function sayFirst(list)
	for _, entry in ipairs(list) do if entry[2] and sayEntry(entry) then return true end end
	return false
end
local function sayAny(list)
	local open = {}
	for _, entry in ipairs(list) do if entry[2] then table.insert(open, entry) end end
	while #open > 0 do
		local index = math.random(#open)
		local entry = table.remove(open, index)
		if sayEntry(entry) then return true end
	end
	return false
end
local function sayOnce(name, extra)
	if watch.said[name] then return false end
	if say(name, extra) then watch.said[name] = true return true end
	return false
end

local function resetRound()
	watch.lifeTaken = 0
	watch.lowest = 1
	watch.low = false
	watch.hold = watch.fraction <= NEAR_DEATH and watch.fraction > 0
end

local function resetMatch()
	watch.sudden, watch.seen = {}, {}
	watch.kills, watch.deaths, watch.taken = 0, 0, 0
	watch.roundKills, watch.roundDeaths = 0, 0
	watch.killStreak, watch.deathStreak, watch.meleeCount = 0, 0, 0
	watch.killTimes, watch.killsOn, watch.said = {}, {}, {}
	watch.matchHits, watch.matchMisses = 0, 0
	watch.matchAt = env.RivalsClock()
	resetRound()
end

P.level = math.max(1, math.floor(tonumber(P.level) or 1))
P.exp = math.max(0, tonumber(P.exp) or 0)

local function gainExp(amount)
	P.exp += amount
	while P.exp >= P.level * 40 do
		P.exp -= P.level * 40
		P.level += 1
		say("levelUp", nil, { level = P.level })
	end
end

local function sendKills(kills)
	local url = env.RivalsLeaderboardUrl
	if type(url) ~= "string" or not url:find("^https://") or kills <= 0 then return end
	local req = request or http_request or (syn and syn.request) or (http and http.request)
	if type(req) ~= "function" then return end
	local okName, name = pcall(function() return game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Name end)
	if not okName or type(name) ~= "string" then return end
	task.spawn(function()
		pcall(req, {
			Url = url .. "/kills", Method = "POST", Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" },
			Body = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode({ name = name, kills = kills }),
		})
	end)
end

local function judgeRound()
	watch.roundKills, watch.roundDeaths = 0, 0
end

local function finishMatch()
	judgeRound()
	sendKills(watch.kills)
	gainExp(10 + 5 * watch.kills)
	local won, lost = watch.kills > watch.deaths, watch.kills < watch.deaths
	if won then watch.winStreak += 1 watch.loseStreak = 0
	elseif lost then watch.loseStreak += 1 watch.winStreak = 0 end
	if watch.kills > 0 and watch.deaths == 0 and watch.taken == 0 then say("winFlawless")
	elseif won and math.random() < 0.4 then say("winRage")
	elseif lost and math.random() < 0.4 then say("loseRage")
	elseif won then sayFirst({ { "winStreak", watch.winStreak >= 2, watch.winStreak }, { "win", true } })
	elseif lost then sayFirst({ { "loseStreak", watch.loseStreak >= 2, watch.loseStreak }, { "lose", true } })
	elseif watch.kills > 0 then say("closeMatch") end
end

local function countSince(times, now, seconds)
	local count = 0
	for _, t in ipairs(times) do if now - t <= seconds then count += 1 end end
	return count
end

local function onKill(name, now)
	watch.kills += 1
	watch.roundKills += 1
	watch.killStreak += 1
	watch.deathStreak = 0
	watch.killsOn[name] = (watch.killsOn[name] or 0) + 1
	table.insert(watch.killTimes, now)
	if #watch.killTimes > 12 then table.remove(watch.killTimes, 1) end
	local record = lastHitOn(name)
	local info = record and record.info or {}
	local weapon = weaponKind(info.weaponName)
	local top = record ~= nil and record.damage ~= nil and record.best ~= nil and record.damage >= 0.9 * record.best
	local sinceStart = now - math.max(watch.roundAt, watch.lastDeathAt)
	local spoken = sayFirst({
		{ "tripleKill", countSince(watch.killTimes, now, 10) >= 3 },
		{ "tenKills", watch.kills == 10 },
		{ "doubleKill", countSince(watch.killTimes, now, 6) >= 2 },
		{ "killStreak5", watch.killStreak == 5 },
		{ "nearDeathSurvive", watch.lowest <= NEAR_DEATH },
		{ "clutchKill", watch.fraction > 0 and watch.fraction <= 0.2 },
		{ "flawless", watch.lifeTaken == 0 },
		{ "killStreak3", watch.killStreak == 3 },
		{ "firstBlood", watch.kills == 1 and watch.deaths == 0 },
		{ "sameTargetAgain", watch.killsOn[name] == 3, name },
	})
	if not spoken then
		sayAny({
			{ "longKill", info.distance ~= nil and info.distance >= 100, info.distance and string.format("%.0f", info.distance) },
			{ "wallKill", record ~= nil and not info.direct and not info.body },
			{ "closeKill", info.distance ~= nil and info.distance < 15 },
			{ "meleeEnemyKill", info.meleeSafe == true or info.escape == true },
			{ "shieldKill", info.flank == true },
			{ "sniperKill", weapon == "sniper" },
			{ "shotgunKill", weapon == "shotgun" },
			{ "pistolKill", weapon == "pistol" },
			{ "explosiveKill", weapon == "explosive" },
			{ "silentKill", info.silent == true },
			{ "bodyShotKill", info.body == true },
			{ "headshotKill", top },
			{ "quickKill", sinceStart <= 5 },
			{ "kill", true },
			{ "kill", true },
		})
	end
	if watch.killStreak >= 3 and math.random() < 0.5 then say("cursePraise") end
	watch.lastKillAt = now
	resetRound()
end

local function onDeath(text, now)
	watch.deaths += 1
	watch.roundDeaths += 1
	watch.deathStreak += 1
	watch.killStreak = 0
	if string.find(text, "급사", 1, true) and watch.target then
		local count = (watch.sudden[watch.target] or 0) + 1
		watch.sudden[watch.target] = count
		if count >= 4 then say("hackConfirm")
		elseif count >= 2 then say("hackSuspect") end
	end
	if watch.deathStreak >= 2 then say("curse") end
	local spoken = sayFirst({
		{ "tradeDeath", now - watch.lastKillAt <= 2 },
		{ "deathStreak3", watch.deathStreak >= 3 },
		{ "meleeDeath", now - watch.lastMeleeAt <= 3 },
		{ "quickDeath", now - math.max(watch.roundAt, watch.lastDeathAt) <= 8 },
		{ "desyncDeath", now - watch.lastDesyncAt <= 1 },
		{ "deathStreak2", watch.deathStreak == 2 },
	})
	if not spoken then sayAny({ { "death", true }, { "death", true }, { "suddenDeath", string.find(text, "급사", 1, true) ~= nil } }) end
	watch.lastDeathAt = now
	resetRound()
end

local function handleEvent(entry)
	local kind = entry.kind
	local text = tostring(entry.text)
	local now = env.RivalsClock()
	watch.lastActivity = now
	if kind == "판 시작" then
		judgeRound()
		resetRound()
		watch.roundAt = now
		sayFirst({ { "matchStartAfterLoss", watch.loseStreak >= 1 }, { "matchStart", true } })
	elseif kind == "킬" then onKill(text, now)
	elseif kind == "피격" then
		watch.lifeTaken += 1
		watch.taken += 1
		watch.lastHurtAt = now
		table.insert(watch.chips, now)
		if #watch.chips > 10 then table.remove(watch.chips, 1) end
		local amount = tonumber(string.match(text, "^(%d+)")) or 0
		local left = tonumber(string.match(text, "남은 (%d+)"))
		if amount >= 50 and (left == nil or left > 0) then say("bigHit", amount)
		elseif amount >= 25 then say("dmgTaken", nil, { dmg = amount })
		elseif amount < 20 and countSince(watch.chips, now, 5) >= 3 then say("chipDamage") end
	elseif kind == "근접 위협" then
		watch.lastMeleeAt = now
		watch.meleeCount += 1
		sayFirst({ { "meleeSwarm", watch.meleeCount == 3 }, { "meleeNear", true } })
	elseif kind == "죽음" then onDeath(text, now)
	elseif kind == "대상" then
		local name = string.match(text, "^(.-) %(")
		if name then
			if watch.sudden[name] and watch.sudden[name] >= 2 then say("hackAgain", nil, { name = name })
			elseif watch.seen[name] and name ~= watch.target then say("enemyReturn", nil, { name = name }) end
			watch.seen[name] = true
		end
		if string.find(text, "근접 무기라 먼저", 1, true) then say("meleePriority")
		elseif name and name ~= watch.target then
			sayFirst({ { "targetBehindWall", string.find(text, "벽 뒤", 1, true) ~= nil }, { "retarget", true, name } })
		end
		watch.target = name
	elseif kind == "디싱크" then
		watch.lastDesyncAt = now
		say("desyncStart")
	elseif kind == "안티에임 이상" then say("antiAimGlitch")
	elseif kind == "훅 오류" then say("hookError") end
end

local function watchHealth()
	local character = Players.LocalPlayer and Players.LocalPlayer.Character
	local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
	if not humanoid then return end
	local maxHealth = humanoid.MaxHealth
	if type(maxHealth) ~= "number" or maxHealth <= 0 or type(humanoid.Health) ~= "number" then return end
	local fraction = humanoid.Health / maxHealth
	watch.fraction = fraction
	if fraction <= 0 then return end
	local now = env.RivalsClock()
	if watch.hold then
		if fraction >= RECOVERED then watch.hold = false end
		watch.wasHurt, watch.lowSince = false, nil
		return
	end
	if fraction < 0.6 then watch.wasHurt = true
	elseif fraction >= 0.999 and watch.wasHurt then watch.wasHurt = false say("healed") end
	if watch.low and fraction <= NEAR_DEATH then
		watch.lowSince = watch.lowSince or now
		if now - watch.lowSince >= 15 then watch.lowSince = now say("lowHpStill") end
	else watch.lowSince = nil end
	if fraction < watch.lowest then watch.lowest = fraction end
	if fraction <= NEAR_DEATH and not watch.low then watch.low = true say("nearDeathWarn")
	elseif watch.low and fraction >= RECOVERED then watch.low = false say("nearDeathSurvive") watch.lowest = 1 end
end

local function watchShots()
	local stats = env.RivalsStats
	local statHits, statMisses = stats and stats.windowHits, stats and stats.windowMisses
	if type(statHits) ~= "number" or type(statMisses) ~= "number" then return end
	if watch.hits == nil or statHits < watch.hits or statMisses < watch.misses then
		watch.hits, watch.misses = statHits, statMisses
		return
	end
	local newMisses, newHits = statMisses - watch.misses, statHits - watch.hits
	watch.hits, watch.misses = statHits, statMisses
	watch.matchHits += newHits
	watch.matchMisses += newMisses
	if newMisses > 0 then
		watch.missStreak += newMisses
		watch.missRun += newMisses
		watch.hitStreak = 0
		watch.hitRun = 0
	end
	if newHits > 0 then
		watch.hitStreak += newHits
		watch.hitRun += newHits
		watch.missStreak = 0
		watch.missRun = 0
	end
	if watch.hitRun >= 10 then
		watch.hitRun = 0 watch.hitStreak = 0 say("hitStreak10")
	elseif watch.hitStreak >= 4 then
		watch.hitStreak = 0 say("hitStreak")
	elseif watch.missRun >= 20 then
		watch.missRun = 0 watch.missStreak = 0 say("missStreak20")
	elseif watch.missStreak >= 8 then
		watch.missStreak = 0 say("missStreak")
	elseif newHits > 0 then sayOnce("firstHit") end
	local finished = watch.matchHits + watch.matchMisses
	if finished >= 30 and watch.matchHits / finished >= 0.75 then sayOnce("accuracyHigh")
	elseif finished >= 40 and watch.matchHits / finished < 0.35 then sayOnce("accuracyLow") end
end

local function watchRecords()
	local stats = env.RivalsStats
	if not stats or type(stats.log) ~= "table" then return end
	local log = stats.log
	local shots = type(stats.shots) == "number" and stats.shots or #log
	if watch.shotsSeen == nil or shots < watch.shotsSeen then watch.shotsSeen = shots return end
	local fresh = math.min(shots - watch.shotsSeen, #log)
	watch.shotsSeen = shots
	local now = env.RivalsClock()
	for index = #log - fresh + 1, #log do
		local record = log[index]
		local info = record.info or {}
		watch.lastActivity = now
		local weapon = info.weaponName
		if weapon and weapon ~= watch.weapon then
			local before = watch.weapon
			watch.weapon = weapon
			if before ~= nil then
				local kind = weaponKind(weapon)
				sayFirst({
					{ "sniperOut", kind == "sniper" }, { "shotgunOut", kind == "shotgun" }, { "explosiveOut", kind == "explosive" },
					{ "weaponSwitch", true, weapon },
				})
			end
		end
		if info.invincible then say("invincibleTarget") end
		if info.block == "deflect" then say("deflectSeen")
		elseif info.block then say("shieldBlocked") end
		if info.escape then say("meleeEscape") end
		if info.distance and info.distance < 10 then say("closeTarget") end
		if info.distance and info.distance >= 100 and record.result == "pending" then
			watch.lastDist = info.distance
			say("hitFar")
		end
		table.insert(watch.pendingRecords, record)
	end
	local keep = {}
	for _, record in ipairs(watch.pendingRecords) do
		if record.result == "pending" then
			if #keep < 400 then table.insert(keep, record) end
		elseif record.result == "hit" then
			local info = record.info or {}
			sayFirst({
				{ "bigDamage", record.damage ~= nil and record.best ~= nil and record.damage >= 50 and record.damage >= 0.9 * record.best, record.damage and string.format("%.0f", record.damage) },
				{ "longShot", info.distance ~= nil and info.distance >= 120, info.distance and string.format("%.0f", info.distance) },
				{ "bodyShot", info.body == true },
				{ "silentHit", info.silent == true },
				{ "wallHit", not info.direct },
			})
		end
	end
	watch.pendingRecords = keep
end

local function watchConfig()
	local now = env.RivalsClock()
	local isOpen = cfg.menuOpen == true
	if isOpen and not watch.open then
		watch.menuAt = now
		say("open")
	elseif not isOpen and watch.open then
		watch.menuAt = nil
		say("menuClose")
	end
	local changed, lastKey, count = false, nil, 0
	for _, key in ipairs(FEATURE_ORDER) do
		local value = cfg[key] == true
		local before = watch.features[key]
		watch.features[key] = value
		if isOpen and watch.open and before ~= nil and before ~= value then
			changed, lastKey, count = true, key, count + 1
		end
	end
	if changed then
		local aimOff = not cfg.rage and not cfg.silent and not cfg.autoFire and not cfg.triggerbot
		local allOn = true
		for _, key in ipairs(FEATURE_ORDER) do if cfg[key] ~= true then allOn = false end end
		local value = cfg[lastKey] == true
		sayFirst({
			{ "allOff", aimOff and not value },
			{ "allOn", allOn and count >= 2 },
			{ "espOff", lastKey == "esp" and not value },
			{ value and "toggleOn" or "toggleOff", true, FEATURE_LABELS[lastKey] },
		})
		watch.menuAt = now
	elseif isOpen and watch.open and watch.menuAt and now - watch.menuAt >= 30 then say("menuLongOpen") end
	local level = math.floor(tonumber(cfg.antiAimLevel) or 1)
	if watch.level ~= nil and level ~= watch.level then
		sayFirst({ { "antiAimMax", level == 4 }, { "antiAimLevel", true, LEVEL_NAMES[level] or tostring(level) } })
	end
	watch.level = level
	local chatter = P.chatter or 2
	if watch.chatter ~= nil and chatter == 3 and watch.chatter ~= 3 then say("chatterMax") end
	watch.chatter = chatter
	watch.open = isOpen
end

local function watchBackground()
	local antiAim = env.RivalsAntiAimStats
	if type(antiAim) == "table" and type(antiAim.sinkTime) == "number" and type(antiAim.shieldTime) == "number" then
		if watch.sink == nil or antiAim.sinkTime < watch.sink then watch.sink = antiAim.sinkTime end
		if watch.shield == nil or antiAim.shieldTime < watch.shield then watch.shield = antiAim.shieldTime end
		if antiAim.sinkTime - watch.sink >= 10 then watch.sink = antiAim.sinkTime say("sinking") end
		if antiAim.shieldTime - watch.shield >= 5 then watch.shield = antiAim.shieldTime say("shieldFacing") end
	end
	local trigger = env.RivalsTrigger
	if type(trigger) == "table" and type(trigger.hits) == "number" then
		if watch.triggerHits ~= nil and trigger.hits > watch.triggerHits then say("triggerFire") end
		watch.triggerHits = trigger.hits
	end
end

local function watchTime()
	local now = env.RivalsClock()
	local hour = hourNow()
	if hour and hour < 5 then say("nightOwl") end
	local minutes = (now - watch.started) / 60
	if minutes >= 60 then say("longSession", tostring(math.floor(minutes))) end
	if (P.chatter or 2) >= 3 and now >= watch.nextTalk then
		watch.nextTalk = now + 120 + math.random(90)
		local finished = watch.matchHits + watch.matchMisses
		local acc = finished > 0 and 100 * watch.matchHits / finished or 0
		sayAny({
			{ "smallTalkKill", watch.kills > 0, { kills = watch.kills } },
			{ "smallTalkDeath", watch.deaths > 0, { deaths = watch.deaths } },
			{ "smallTalkAcc", finished >= 10 },
			{ "smallTalkPing", true },
			{ "smallTalkMatch", true },
			{ "smallTalkSelf", true },
			{ "confidence", finished >= 5 and acc >= 70 },
			{ "doubt", finished >= 10 and acc < 40 },
		})
	end
	if watch.matchId ~= nil then
		if watch.deaths - watch.kills >= 2 and not cfg.rage then say("suggestRage") end
		if watch.deaths >= 3 and not cfg.antiAim then say("suggestAntiAim") end
		if watch.deaths >= 3 and cfg.antiAim and math.floor(tonumber(cfg.antiAimLevel) or 1) <= 2 then say("suggestAntiAimLevel") end
		if watch.kills >= 1 and now - math.max(watch.lastHurtAt, watch.matchAt) >= 90 then say("untouched") end
		if now - watch.matchAt >= 300 then say("matchLong") end
		if watch.lastActivity > 0 and now - watch.lastActivity >= 120 then say("idleLong") end
	end
end

local function poll()
	local events = env.RivalsEvents
	if events and events.list then
		if events.id ~= watch.matchId or watch.cursor > #events.list then
			if watch.matchId ~= nil then finishMatch() end
			watch.matchId = events.id
			watch.matchCount += 1
			watch.cursor = 0
			resetMatch()
		end
		local list = events.list
		for index = watch.cursor + 1, #list do handleEvent(list[index]) end
		watch.cursor = #list
	end
	watchHealth()
	watchShots()
	watchRecords()
	watchConfig()
	watchBackground()
	local now = env.RivalsClock()
	if (P.chatter or 2) >= 3 then
		if watch.nextIdle == 0 then watch.nextIdle = now + 50 + math.random(40)
		elseif now >= watch.nextIdle then watch.nextIdle = now + 50 + math.random(40) say("idle") end
	end
	watchTime()
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- 입력 / 루프
----------------------------------------------------------------
petButton.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if pressed(input) and not drag then
		local position = input.Position
		drag = { px = position.X, py = position.Y, x0 = pos.x, y0 = pos.y, moved = false }
	end
end)

table.insert(connections, UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
	if not drag or not moving(input) then return end
	local position = input.Position
	local dx, dy = position.X - drag.px, position.Y - drag.py
	if not drag.moved and math.abs(dx) + math.abs(dy) < 5 then return end
	drag.moved = true
	pos.x = drag.x0 + dx
	pos.y = drag.y0 + dy
	applyPet()
end))

table.insert(connections, UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
	if not drag or not pressed(input) then return end
	local dragged = drag.moved
	drag = nil
	if dragged then
		P.x, P.y = pos.x, pos.y
		touch()
		say("petMoved")
	else
		if menu and menu.Toggle then menu.Toggle() end
		local now = env.RivalsClock()
		table.insert(watch.pokes, now)
		if #watch.pokes > 6 then table.remove(watch.pokes, 1) end
		sayFirst({ { "pokeSpam", countSince(watch.pokes, now, 6) >= 5 }, { "poke", true } })
	end
end))

local clock, slow = 0, 0
local lastWidth, lastHeight = viewport()
table.insert(connections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
	clock += dt or 0
	slow += dt or 0
	if slow < 0.1 then return end
	local step = slow
	slow = 0
	gui.Enabled = P.show == true
	if not gui.Enabled then return end
	local now = env.RivalsClock()
	if bubble.Visible and now >= hideAt then hideBubble() end
	if hop > 0 then hop = math.max(0, hop - step * 3) end
	bobOffset = math.sin(clock * 2.4) * 2 - math.sin(hop * math.pi) * 10
	petButton.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(pos.x, pos.y + bobOffset)
	local w, h = viewport()
	if w ~= lastWidth or h ~= lastHeight then
		lastWidth, lastHeight = w, h
		applyPet()
	end
end))

local pollClock = 0
table.insert(connections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
	pollClock += dt or 0
	if pollClock < 0.25 then return end
	pollClock = 0
	poll()
end))

placePet()
for _, key in ipairs(FEATURE_ORDER) do watch.features[key] = cfg[key] == true end
watch.open = cfg.menuOpen == true
local startEvents = env.RivalsEvents
if startEvents and startEvents.list then
	watch.matchId = startEvents.id
	watch.cursor = #startEvents.list
end
watch.matchAt = env.RivalsClock()
do
	local hour = hourNow()
	sayFirst({
		{ "greetMorning", hour ~= nil and hour >= 5 and hour < 11 },
		{ "greetNight", hour ~= nil and (hour >= 23 or hour < 5) },
		{ "greet", true },
	})
end

env.RivalsPet = {
	gui = gui,
	position = pos,
	settings = P,
	Say = function(_, name, extra) return say(name, extra) end,
	Situations = SITUATIONS,
	Soften = soften,
	ResetPosition = function()
		P.x, P.y = nil, nil
		placePet()
		touch()
	end,
}

env.RivalsPetUnload = function()
	for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do connection:Disconnect() end
	env.RivalsPet = nil
	gui:Destroy()
end

end)
